## EPM:EPM-051

When comparability fails, narrow the claim instead of normalising away inconvenient differences.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Performance comparisons across people, units, time periods, demographic groups or process variants.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Report separate descriptive distributions or refrain from ranking rather than fit an unjustified adjustment model.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Relevant context variables, sufficient subgroup support and a clear target estimand; sensitive attributes require particular justification.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/16.md#r-168b3c8ee8ee58a8f7eb6826da83ca57e793c94a619861104d1873777985e0b9">Complete shared assumptions · 168b3c8ee8ee</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst identifies comparability limits; affected participants and accountable decision makers assess the meaning and acceptable use. <a id="EPM-EPM-052"></a>
## EPM:EPM-052

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-021.md#EPM-EPM-052">EPM:EPM-052</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-052</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Simulation as conditional scenario analysis</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM6</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S001</p></li><li><p>EPM-S027</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>DOCUMENTED_IMPORT_OR_HYBRID</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/9b.md#r-9bd39bf038b762b96e7b05cfce84d7a5627ea033591561cb5ea829c29a71804a">Complete shared evidence limit · 9bd39bf038b7</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Conditional simulation scenarios and counterfactual interpretation</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A scenario result states which assumptions generate the advantage and what prospective evidence would be needed before wider use.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Use simulation to explore consequences under stated process, resource, arrival and intervention assumptions; compare baseline reproduction and sensitivity before drawing a conditional scenario conclusion. Keep calibration evidence separate from intervention validation.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>The decision concerns a counterfactual change that cannot yet be tested directly and a model can represent its relevant mechanism.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A transparent analytical bound or small controlled pilot can dominate an elaborate simulator; no simulation is necessary for an already observable factual question.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>A specified scenario model, relevant calibration evidence, intervention representation and sensitivity analysis.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/58.md#r-58fc19879812a3c505e4dda2789f15aa8be566339389181332e07cd235923170">Complete shared assumptions · 58fc19879812</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst constructs scenarios; authorised decision makers choose whether and how to test them in reality. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Exchange traceable intervals and interaction patterns with operational participants; distinguish observed facts, proposed causes, agreed actions and consequences. Specific receiving decision: Analyst constructs scenarios; authorised decision makers choose whether and how to test them in reality.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst constructs scenarios; authorised decision makers choose whether and how to test them in reality.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A scenario result states which assumptions generate the advantage and what prospective evidence would be needed before wider use.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A what-if scenario produces a favourable result and is treated as evidence that the same intervention will work in the organisation.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Good baseline fit does not identify correct counterfactual responses. SCOPE’s simulation/semi-synthetic evaluation does not establish real deployed causal benefit.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/3d.md#r-3d99ffdf1bbd91db97d92fc2e32f2995339f9fe7c3f7ccd97d989e1594e6f822">Complete shared criticism · 3d99ffdf1bbd</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-011.md#EPM-EPM-S001">EPM:EPM-S001</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S027">EPM:EPM-S027</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R036</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R037</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R049</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R059</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>A transparent analytical bound or small controlled pilot can dominate an elaborate simulator; no simulation is necessary for an already observable factual question.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. <a id="EPM-EPM-055"></a>
## EPM:EPM-055

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-021.md#EPM-EPM-055">EPM:EPM-055</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-055</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Drift detection with latency and false-alarm costs</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM7</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S037</p></li><li><p>EPM-S021</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/0d.md#r-0df17a012e18f614d902512222a4cef03df16762c7aae8eea15c3d411b15b1cc">Complete shared evidence limit · 0df17a012e18</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Time-regime-bound logs/models/references, drift and streaming/adaptation claims</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>An alert carries its feature basis and uncertainty, and the response distinguishes detection from diagnosis.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Choose observable features and a detector matched to the relevant kind of change; evaluate sensitivity, false alarms, detection delay and resource costs under plausible stable and changed conditions. Treat an alarm as evidence for investigation, not proof of a particular cause.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>The process may change in a way that invalidates a model or decision and timely detection would matter.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A scheduled comparison of a few interpretable indicators can suffice for slow or low-consequence change.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Baseline/window semantics, candidate drift types, alert costs and a consumer able to investigate.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/ef.md#r-ef2ecbb6673296aa6b05b4a26a03aedd3e710b46a738bf091d9f6988afb1b1d5">Complete shared assumptions · ef2ecbb66732</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst defines and evaluates detection; operational/model authorities decide what a confirmed change means. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Communicate alarms, source/model version changes, retained history and alternative change explanations to actors able to investigate and revise. Specific receiving decision: Analyst defines and evaluates detection; operational/model authorities decide what a confirmed change means.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst defines and evaluates detection; operational/model authorities decide what a confirmed change means.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>An alert carries its feature basis and uncertainty, and the response distinguishes detection from diagnosis.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A detector is selected without knowing which changes it can detect, its delay or the cost of false alarms.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>The 2023 benchmark shows detector performance varies by drift characteristics. A single threshold cannot guarantee useful detection across all processes.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/07.md#r-074c3e35efd92e08f774611a4b11fe398d80c478445cf3e339d632c10a585a9f">Complete shared criticism · 074c3e35efd9</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S021">EPM:EPM-S021</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-015.md#EPM-EPM-S037">EPM:EPM-S037</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-017.md#EPM-EPM-S060">EPM:EPM-S060</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R039</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R040</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R043</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>A scheduled comparison of a few interpretable indicators can suffice for slow or low-consequence change.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><p>Rare changes, interacting drifts and nonstationary recording may defeat calibrated thresholds.</p></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/9b.md#r-9bf8f2e6d4ea056736f21c6ebe225c444f2b0635ee5e415233224c10e682e570">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · 9bf8f2e6d4ea</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Retain drift detection as a scoped monitoring capability with explicit delay/false-alarm trade-offs. <dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-021.md#EPM-EPM-057">EPM:EPM-057</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-057</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Streaming with explicit forgetting and incomplete-case semantics</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM7</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S034</p></li><li><p>EPM-S052</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>CONTEXT_DEPENDENT</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/a0.md#r-a0a355daad8780e6f0561fa333ecc110e4e66d89e68bfc730449d54045447521">Complete shared evidence limit · a0a355daad87</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Time-regime-bound logs/models/references, drift and streaming/adaptation claims</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>An online result states what history it represents and how partial/late observations affect it.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Specify window/forgetting policy, event arrival order, late-data handling and prefix semantics; expose the trade-off between latency, memory and historical coverage. Assess partial-case conformance as partial rather than pretending the case has ended.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Data volume or required latency makes bounded online processing preferable to complete batch analysis.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Periodic batch analysis with explicit windows is adequate when near-real-time decisions are unnecessary.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Event-stream semantics, resource budget, state retention policy and support for incomplete cases.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/2b.md#r-2b04b7f4c0fd1069a241a41e5f413e7e73875f8afa8963e2cede71c3ae3fd4cf">Complete shared assumptions · 2b04b7f4c0fd</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Stream analyst or service operator discloses retention and delay to the decision consumer. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Communicate alarms, source/model version changes, retained history and alternative change explanations to actors able to investigate and revise. Specific receiving decision: Stream analyst or service operator discloses retention and delay to the decision consumer.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Stream analyst or service operator discloses retention and delay to the decision consumer.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>An online result states what history it represents and how partial/late observations affect it.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>An online model silently forgets older behaviour or treats incomplete running cases as finished deviations.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Forgetting can erase infrequent but important obligations; model churn can exceed operational capacity. An absence-of-deployment statement from 2018 is not a 2026 finding.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/42.md#r-4248eb3729464744550700c9e2444d2ef026a4a753149c20cc374638a432f46b">Complete shared criticism · 4248eb372946</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S021">EPM:EPM-S021</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-015.md#EPM-EPM-S034">EPM:EPM-S034</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-017.md#EPM-EPM-S052">EPM:EPM-S052</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R017</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R039</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R042</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R043</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R059</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Periodic batch analysis with explicit windows is adequate when near-real-time decisions are unnecessary.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><p>End-to-end usefulness depends on whether timely action is feasible, not only processing throughput.</p></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/bf.md#r-bfebaa27133a947f5a4944fb7935bec4d2ddd6d910f1c97b6f9fd6dba9af30d4">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · bfebaa27133a</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Retain streaming as a latency/resource configuration, not the compulsory successor to batch mining.
